Verified: `node --check` clean, new `test/invoice-review-bulk.test.mjs` (18 assertions: correct per-bill row writes at the right sheet row, per-item error isolation — one bad bill_id in a batch doesn't sink the rest, dedup on re-approve, constant write-call-count regardless of batch size), `test/invoice-no-bill.test.mjs` updated for the `invBuildApprovalPayload` extraction, full suite 26/28 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures — `pricing-model`, `scope-core` — confirmed via `git stash` against unmodified `main`). **Not yet used live** — first check: on Review Bills, tap "Select multiple," check 2-3 priced bills, tap "Approve selected," confirm the total shown matches, and confirm all selected cards fade out without a manual refresh.

**97. `ensureColumns` now grows a sheet's grid before writing a new header — fixes "Range exceeds grid limits" on Save (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.6`; worker.js `ensureColumns`).** Brett hit `Error: Sheets API error on POST /values:batchUpdate: Invalid data[0]: Range (Work_Orders!AO1) exceeds grid limits. Max rows: 998, max columns: 40` trying to Save Changes on a WO edit (adding/editing the checklist). Root cause: **Work_Orders' actual Google Sheet grid is capped at 40 columns** (`gridProperties.columnCount`, set whenever the tab was created/duplicated) — a hard limit completely separate from how many named columns the code thinks the tab has. `ensureColumns` computes where to write a new header purely from the widest existing row (`width + i`) and never checked whether the sheet's grid was actually wide enough to hold it. Column 41 (`AO`) is exactly one past the 40-column cap, so the very first time a NEW column landed there — here, `Checklist`, via `POST /wo/checklist`'s own `ensureColumns` call, on a WO whose edit was the first save to actually need that column written — the write didn't get truncated or silently dropped, it failed outright and took the whole Save with it. This is a systemic gap, not a Work_Orders-only one: ANY tab hits this the moment its real column count crosses whatever grid width it happened to start with. Fixed generally: before writing new headers, `ensureColumns` now reads the sheet's actual `gridProperties.columnCount` (`GET ?fields=sheets.properties(sheetId,title,gridProperties)`) and, if the grid is narrower than what's about to be written, first grows it via `updateSheetProperties` (+20 columns of headroom, so the next new field doesn't hit the same wall again). Best-effort — wrapped in try/catch so a metadata-read hiccup falls through to the original write and its real error, rather than masking it. Verified: `node --check` clean, `test/ensure-columns.test.mjs` (9 assertions covering placement, blank-header skip, multi-column, empty tab) still green — the mock's `sheetsRequest` returns the same stub for every call regardless of method, so the new metadata GET resolves to `{sheets: undefined}` → the resize block no-ops → existing behavior unchanged when the grid check can't run, which is exactly the fallback this needs. Full suite 24/26 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures). **Not yet used live** — first check: retry editing/saving WO-1133 (or any WO whose checklist save previously errored) and confirm it now succeeds without the grid-limit error.

**96. "Fix email" button added to the Send & Track board — the rule 95 fix, made self-serve (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.5`; index.html `stRow`/`fixInvoiceEmail`).** Brett screenshotted Send & Track right after rule 95 shipped and correctly called out that it still showed "no email" on the same 3 trash invoices. Rule 95 fixed the CODE — new trash invoices now get an email, and the backfill tools stopped lying about writes succeeding — but code fixes aren't retroactive: an invoice already sitting in QuickBooks with a blank `BillEmail` stays blank until something actually runs the backfill against it, and that required opening a second page (`qb-invoice-email-backfill.html`) that hadn't been re-run yet. Rather than just pointing Brett at that other page again, moved the actual fix onto the board itself: any "no email" row now gets a **"Fix email"** button (`fixInvoiceEmail(id, btn)`) right next to the disabled Send button, calling the same `/qb/backfill-invoice-emails` endpoint scoped to just that one invoice id — resolves the email from the invoice's customer or the owner above it in QuickBooks, verifies the write actually stuck (same check rule 95 added), and refreshes the row. Reports plainly when it can't fix something: no email anywhere up the QuickBooks chain, or "Bill with parent" silently blocking the write. Verified: `node --check` clean, inline scripts in index.html pass, full suite 24/26 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures — untouched by this change). **Not yet used live** — first check: tap "Fix email" on 151 W Lanvale St #1652 / 115 W 29th St #1651 / 153 W Lanvale St #1654 on Send & Track and confirm each either gets an email (Send lights up) or shows a specific reason why not.

**95. QB email backfill tools now verify a write actually stuck + surface "Bill with parent" + trash line description cleaned up + button-contrast sweep (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.4`; worker.js `qbBackfillEmails`/`qbBackfillInvoiceEmails`/`qbListEntities`/`buildTrashInvoiceLines`; qb-email-backfill.html, qb-invoice-email-backfill.html, index.html, tenant.html, owner.html).** Follow-up to rule 94. Brett: repeatedly using "Force the parent's email" on specific customers (e.g. two properties billing through Phoenix Estate Rentals) and having them keep reappearing in the backfill preview like nothing was ever written; also generally "not going to QB / sending to owner" for trash invoices. Two separate fixes: (1) **Verify-after-write.** `qbBackfillEmails` and `qbBackfillInvoiceEmails` used to treat any 200 response with a `Customer`/`Invoice` object back as success. QuickBooks can accept a sparse `PrimaryEmailAddr` update and hand back an object that still doesn't carry the new email — this happens on a sub-customer with **"Bill with parent" turned on**, where QuickBooks routes invoice emails to the PARENT's contact regardless of what's set on the sub's own email field. Both endpoints now compare the email QuickBooks actually returns against what was requested, and only report "updated" on a real match — a mismatch now surfaces a specific, actionable error naming "Bill with parent" as the likely cause instead of a false ✅. (2) **Surfaced `BillWithParent` from QuickBooks** (`qbListEntities` now requests it; `qbResolveEmailBackfill` carries it through) as a "bills via parent" badge next to any customer in `qb-email-backfill.html`'s "to set" and "already has an email" tables — visible BEFORE Brett even tries a force-fix, not just after it silently fails. **If a Force keeps "not sticking," the real fix is unchecking "Bill with parent" on that customer in QuickBooks (Customer → Edit) or fixing the email on the correct parent — the tool can't override a QuickBooks setting that's actively working against it.** Separately: (3) **Trash invoice line description** — Brett wants no address on customer-facing invoice lines (matches the existing generic-item design already documented for this exact reason); `buildTrashInvoiceLines`' base line now reads exactly **"Trash Service"** (was `"Trash service — <date>"`); the invoice's own transaction date already carries the date, so nothing is lost. Extra-charge lines are untouched (still name the reason + date — no address in either). (4) **Button contrast sweep** — rule 86 (Aug 13) set the "white text on every colored button" convention Hub-wide, but it never reached these two standalone QB tools (`.btn`/`.btn.success`/`#force-btn`/`#ground-btn` all had `color:#1b1f24` — literally black text on blue/green/red) or a few stragglers that were added to index.html afterward (the Send & Track chips, two "Link" buttons on QB Mapping, one bill-receipt toggle) and to `tenant.html`/`owner.html` (`.btn-green`, `owner.html`'s `.btn-blue`). All now `color:#fff`, consistent with rule 86. Verified: `node --check` clean on worker.js, all inline `<script>` blocks in index.html/tenant.html/owner.html/vendor.html/both QB tools pass, full suite 24/26 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures), `test/qb-email-backfill.test.mjs` and `test/trash.test.mjs` green (the new `bill_with_parent` field is additive, doesn't touch any existing assertion). **Not yet used live** — first check: re-run `qb-invoice-email-backfill.html` for the 3 stuck trash invoices (115 W 29th St #1651, 151 W Lanvale St #1652, 153 W Lanvale St #1654) and confirm they leave "Not sent"; re-preview `qb-email-backfill.html` and check whether 1106 N Bond St / 1110 N Dukeland St now show a "bills via parent" badge — if so, that's confirmed as the actual blocker and needs a QuickBooks-side setting change, not another Force click.

**94. Trash-service invoices weren't getting a send-to email, same gap rule 60 fixed elsewhere (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.3`; worker.js `trashInvoice`).** Brett's report: trash-service invoices "not making it to QuickBooks," erroring with something about a missing email address even though the properties have an email in QuickBooks. Root cause: `trashInvoice`'s POST payload to QuickBooks (`CustomerRef`/`Line`/`TxnDate`/`PrivateNote`) never set `BillEmail` at all — the exact gap rule 60 (FEATURE_LOG, main Hub invoice flow, ~line 7789) already documented and fixed months ago: **QuickBooks does not copy a customer's saved email onto an API-created invoice.** The main invoice-creation path learned this and now reads the email and stamps `BillEmail` before posting; `trashInvoice` was a separate, newer code path that never got the same fix. `Trash_Properties` has no email field of its own (only `QBO_Customer_ID`), so the fix does a `GET customer/{id}` from QuickBooks right before posting and sets `BillEmail` from `PrimaryEmailAddr.Address` — same validation (100-char limit + basic regex) and same never-block-the-invoice retry (if QuickBooks rejects the address, delete `BillEmail` and repost once, warning Brett rather than failing the whole invoice) as the main flow. Also added the same "no email on file" warning to the **preview** step (before Send is even tapped), not just the send/confirm path, so Brett sees it up front. **If invoices were already failing outright with a fault, this should stop that entirely; if they were posting but silently landing with no send-to (so QuickBooks never emailed the customer), those still-open invoices in QuickBooks will need their `BillEmail` set by hand once — the existing `qb-invoice-email-backfill.html` tool (built for the exact same gap on the main flow) works for these too since it queries QuickBooks directly, not this repo's own tabs.** Verified: `node --check` clean, full test suite 24/26 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures), `test/trash.test.mjs` (pure billing-math tests, untouched by this change) still green. **Not yet used live** — first check: preview an unbilled trash property/week and confirm no "no email" warning if the QB customer has one; send it and confirm the resulting invoice in QuickBooks shows a filled-in Customer Email field.

**93. Tenant portal no longer shows work orders that predate the tenant's move-in (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.2`; worker.js `isBackgroundWO`/`isTenantNotifiable`/`tenantWorkorders`; index.html `openWODetail`).** Brett's report: tenant "Matt" at 151 W Lanvale St Apt 2 could see a turnover-cleaning WO in his portal that was opened right around when he was moving in — background work tied to the unit being prepped for him, not something he needed to know about. Root cause: `isTenantNotifiable` (governs SMS) already skipped a WO whose `Created_Date` is before the tenant's `Move_In_Date`, but `tenantWorkorders` (governs what the tenant PORTAL lists) never applied that same check — it only filtered on `Tenant_Visible`, `Property_ID`, and unit/tenant-ID match. So a pre-move-in WO wouldn't text a new tenant but would still be sitting in their portal if they opened it. The isTenantNotifiable comment literally called this out in advance ("Three near-copies of this check is why one of them ended up not being applied") — this was that. Fixed by extracting the date comparison into one shared helper, **`isBackgroundWO(tenant, wo)`**, called from both `isTenantNotifiable` and `tenantWorkorders`'s filter — so the SMS gate and the portal-visibility gate can't drift apart again. This is fully automatic: no admin action needed, applies retroactively to every existing WO on next portal load, and covers every tenant/unit going forward, not just Matt's case. **Known limit:** there's no override to force a background WO to show anyway (e.g. if Brett ever wants a new tenant to see pre-move-in repair history for transparency) — if that's ever needed, ask for a force-show flag; not built now since Brett's ask was specifically to hide, not to selectively reveal. Separately, the Hub's existing **"Show to Tenant" toggle** (WO detail → Tenant Access Settings, already wired to `Tenant_Visible` → `/wo/set-tenant-visibility`, this was NOT new — it already fully hid a WO from every tenant on request) now (a) labels itself with the actual current tenant's name ("Show to Matt" instead of generic "Show to Tenant") so it reads as the manual "remove this tenant from this WO" action Brett asked for, and (b) shows a "🚫 Auto-hidden from [name]" banner in that same panel when the new automatic date rule is why a WO won't appear, so "why isn't Matt seeing this" has a visible answer without checking code. Note: `/wo-tenants`, `/wo-tenant/add`, `/wo-tenant/remove` (a separate `WO_Tenants` link table) already existed in worker.js pre-dating this session but are dead code — nothing in the frontend calls them and `tenantWorkorders` never consulted that table for visibility; left as-is, not part of this fix, flagged here so a future session doesn't assume they're load-bearing. Verified: `node --check` clean on worker.js and both index.html script blocks, full test suite still 24/26 (same 2 pre-existing unrelated failures), `test/tenant-privacy.test.mjs` green. **Not yet used live** — first check: open a WO created before a current tenant's Move_In_Date in that tenant's portal (or re-check Matt's actual turnover-cleaning WO) and confirm it's gone from the list; open the same WO in the Hub and confirm the "Show to [name]" label and (if it predates move-in) the auto-hidden banner both show.

**92. Vendor bug-report fixes — photo album/lightbox, upload retry, invoice-file upload, invoice autosave, tenant/owner vendor contact (Aug 17, Worker `2026-08-17.1`; index.html + vendor.html + tenant.html + worker.js).** Five fixes from one Brett bug report + two live incidents (Oscar/Philadelphia Rd upload failure, Eddie/Gladden Ave photo access). (1) **Photo lightbox** — before this, every photo/video rendered as a bare `<a target=_blank>` chip split into separate before/after/report/receipt lists; no gallery/lightbox/carousel component existed in the codebase at all (grepped clean). Tapping one bounced to Google Drive's own single-file viewer, which is the literal source of "can only go back and forth from one photo." Now `loadPortalAttachments` builds a combined `_rcWoItems[woId]` array across all types in a stable order and renders real `<img>` thumbnails (`https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=`); tapping any thumbnail opens `rcOpenLightbox` — a full-screen in-app viewer that swipes/arrow-keys through the WO's ENTIRE photo+video set as one album, not per-type. Non-image/video files (PDF receipts, pre-`Drive_File_ID` legacy rows) fall back to the old link chip. Videos play inline via `<video>`; a "won't play? open in Drive" fallback link covers Drive's spotty direct-video-stream support. Identical implementation in index.html (Hub) and vendor.html (portal), index.html's version also keeps its existing per-file delete button (now an overlay "×" on the thumbnail corner instead of a separate chip). (2) **Upload retry** — `_uploadOneFile` PUTs the file bytes directly from the browser to a Google Drive resumable-upload URL, with **zero retry** before this fix; one dropped packet on a flaky job-site cellular connection silently failed the whole photo with no recovery but re-picking the file. Now retries up to 3 attempts total (each attempt requests a fresh upload session rather than trying to resume a partial byte-range — simpler and always correct), 1.2s/2.4s backoff between tries, and a manual "Retry" tap if all 3 fail. This is the strongest available explanation for Oscar's Philadelphia Rd failure — Cowork has no access to live Worker logs to confirm the exact failure mode, but architecturally this was the only unrecovered failure point in the whole upload path. (3) **Vendor invoice file upload** (`worker.js addVendorBill`, `vendor.html` bill modal) — vendors had a text box for their invoice *number* (`Vendor_Invoice_No`) but literally no control to attach the invoice document/photo itself; only receipts had an upload button. Added `uploadBillInvoiceFile` (same `/upload-photo` path, `file_type:'invoice'`, routes to the private `_Internal — Vendor Bills` folder per `NON_SHARE_FILE_TYPES`, never customer-shared) → `Invoice_File_URL`/`Invoice_File_ID` on the submitted bill (`ensureColumns` before `addRow`, same pattern as rule-1917's `Vendor_Invoice_No` fix). Surfaced on the Hub's Review Bills card (`irBillContext` → new `vendorInvLine`) — the invoice NUMBER was captured on submit but was never shown back to Brett anywhere either, that's fixed too. Also fixed while in `openBillModal`: `bill-invoice-no` was never reset between jobs, so a prior job's invoice number could silently carry over into the next bill. (4) **Invoice-field autosave** (index.html `invBuilderHtml`) — `saveDraftInvoice` already existed and worked, but nothing called it except an explicit "💾 Save draft" tap; typing a Customer Charge or Invoice Memo then clicking away (another WO, the modal backdrop, a nav link) before tapping Save silently discarded it with zero warning. Both `inv-charge-`+k and `inv-memo-`+k now carry `onblur="saveDraftInvoice(k)"` — blur fires before the click that moves focus away completes, so this catches the exact "accidentally clicked away" case Brett hit. The manual button stays. (5) **Tenant/owner vendor contact** (`worker.js enrichWO`) — tenant.html (line ~390) and owner.html (line ~650) BOTH already had a "Technician"/"Vendor" detail row template reading `wo.vendor_name`, but `enrichWO`'s `base` object never actually resolved `Vendor_ID` to a name for ANY caller — line 1767 even did `base.vendor_phone = ''` on a field that was never set in the first place. Both portals had silently shown "—" forever. Fixed by threading an optional `opts.vendors` (the Vendors tab, fetched once per request in `tenantWorkorders`/`ownerWorkorders`) into `enrichWO`, which resolves `vendor_name`/`vendor_phone`/`vendor_trade` from `wo.Vendor_ID` when present. **Tenants get all three** (name+phone+trade, `tel:` link in tenant.html) so they can coordinate access directly with whoever's assigned. **Owners get name+trade only** — phone deliberately withheld (new `base.vendor_phone = ''` under `opts.ownerView`) to keep the vendor relationship mediated through Brett rather than letting owners negotiate with vendors directly. Callers that don't pass `opts.vendors` (the vendor's own `vendorWorkorders` view, generic admin reads) are unaffected — `vendorName` stays `''` exactly as before. **🔴 Separately flagged, not code-fixable from Cowork:** Eddie's "can't access photos on Gladden Ave" is very likely the still-unactioned Aug-8 photo-sharing backfill (rule 56) — pre-fix media stayed private to the service account and the one-tap `POST /admin/share-attachments` ("Share them all") backfill has never been run (confirmed by grepping CURRENT.md/FEATURE_LOG/BACKLOG — every mention is still "one tap left for Brett"); needs Brett's tap in the live Hub, no tool available to any Cowork session can grant Drive sharing. Verified: all inline `<script>` blocks in index.html/vendor.html/tenant.html plus worker.js pass `node --check`; full test suite 24/26 green (the 2 failures — pricing-model, scope-core — are pre-existing on unmodified `main`, unrelated, need env vars this sandbox lacks). **Not yet used live by Brett** — first check: tap a photo thumbnail on a WO with multiple photos and confirm it swipes; submit a vendor bill with an invoice file and confirm it shows on Review Bills; type an invoice memo, click away without saving, reopen and confirm it stuck; open a tenant/owner login on a WO with a vendor assigned and confirm the Technician row is populated.

**91. Pricing constants moved to a private store — public repo no longer carries the markup math (Aug 13, `2026-08-13.5`; worker.js + index.html; rule 73 remediation).** The tiered/itemized/on-site/card-fee constants were hard-coded in `worker.js calcTieredEstimate` AND mirrored in the Hub client `index.html` (invPricing, the Review-Bills `ir-` calculator, calcTieredEstimate mirror) — meaning the pricing formula was downloadable from the public Pages JS and browsable in the repo. Now all constants live ONLY in a private store: the Cloudflare secret **`PRICING_CONFIG`** (JSON) or the private Config-sheet row **`pricing_config`** (dual-source, same pattern as `receipt_customer_cards`). Shape is documented (generically) at `getPricingConfig` in worker.js. New admin-gated **`GET /pricing-config`** (WORKER_SECRET only — NOT in any role scope, so vendors/owners/tenants can't read it) returns the config to the Hub, which fetches it once in `loadAll()` into `PRICING_CFG`. All three client pricing paths + the worker estimate path are now **config-driven with NO literals in source**; suggestion labels build their numbers from the fetched config at runtime. **Dormancy:** if the config is unset, `calcTieredEstimate` returns null → Tiered/Itemized suggestions + `generateEstimateText` are OFF (a clear note shows); **pass-through (at cost) and manual pricing still work**, so invoicing never fully breaks. **Correctness proof:** a 7,935-case sweep asserted the new config-driven math is byte-identical to the old hard-coded formula across all three engines. **🔴 REQUIRED to restore pricing suggestions:** Brett must set `PRICING_CONFIG` (Cloudflare secret) or the Config `pricing_config` row with the real constants — until then suggestions/estimates are dormant. **Rule going forward:** never reintroduce a pricing constant ($admin fee, markup %, card fee, hourly) as a literal in worker.js or any client file — read it from `getPricingConfig` / `PRICING_CFG`.

**90. Scope Creator workflow — notes → Scope of Work → WO (request for estimate) → capture estimate → customer proposal (Aug 13, `2026-08-13.4`; worker.js `scope*` module + 13 `/scope/*` routes; new `Scopes` tab; `scope-creator.html` + hub button + 🧰 TOOLS entry; `test/scope-core.test.mjs`).** B-030/031/076. New self-provisioning **`Scopes`** tab (`SCOPES_HEADERS`, numeric ID). Endpoints: GET `/scopes`,`/scope?id=`,`/scope/drive-list`; POST `/scope/create`,`/ingest`,`/generate`,`/command`,`/update`,`/split`,`/approve`,`/to-wo`,`/estimate`,`/proposal` (all admin-secret gated — no new token). **Input** three ways: typed/voice text, an uploaded handwriting photo, or a file picked from the handwriting scan Drive folder (`SCOPE_SCAN_FOLDER_DEFAULT` = `1iXjjwsnPKF_GtlR8gesxS9uJppO4xntZ`, overridable via Config `scope_scan_folder_id`). OCR **reuses the Receipt-Reconciler Claude-vision pattern** — `scopeOcr` = `bytesToB64`+Anthropic `claude-sonnet-4-6` (image or PDF), transcription-only. `scope/generate` organizes Raw_Input into structured line items JSON (area/trade/description/qty/note, **no prices**); `scope/command` applies a natural-language edit ("remove the plumbing section") returning revised items + a change summary; `scope/split` carves selected items into a child scope (own vendor). **`scope/to-wo`** calls the real `createWorkOrder` (Type `estimate`, line items rendered into Description + Checklist), then `updateWOFields` Status→**`Estimate Requested`** + `Scope_ID` (ensureColumns, best-effort), and **re-keys any scope-staged photo Attachments** (uploaded under `wo_id='SCOPE-<id>'` via the standard create-upload-session→PUT→log-attachment chain) onto the new WO as before-photos. `scope/estimate` records the vendor's estimate #/amount/notes on the scope (editable afterward). **`scope/proposal` builds the CUSTOMER doc from scope+estimate via `calcTieredEstimate` — markup applied SERVER-SIDE, and ONLY `final_price`/`deposit` are ever returned or stored; no cost/markupPct/step math leaves the function (hard rule Aug 10).** `scope-creator.html` is an admin tool (mh_auth) carrying NO pricing math; voice = Web Speech API. Verified: `node --check` clean, `test/scope-core.test.mjs` 13 assertions (item-clean, markup applied, **no-leak contract**), full suite 26/26 green, client grepped clean of cost/markup. **Not yet run against live Sheets** — first use: create a scope, organize, approve, Create WO, confirm it lands as "Estimate Requested" with the checklist + photos, then add an estimate and generate the proposal (verify the customer total shows, no cost). NOTE: pick-from-scan-folder needs the `maintenance-hub-sheets@…` service account shared on that Drive folder (same requirement as rule 85), else the list is empty.

**89. Itemized WO checklist — vendor checks items off, gated completion (Aug 13, `2026-08-13.3`; index.html + vendor.html + worker.js `saveChecklist`; `Work_Orders.Checklist`).** Optional per-WO checklist defined on the New/Edit WO forms (one item per line), stored as JSON on **`Work_Orders.Checklist`** (self-provisioned via `ensureColumns` — createWorkOrder calls it BEFORE reading the header row so the field isn't dropped, FL rule 37). New **`POST /wo/checklist`** → `saveChecklist(env,body)` (ensureColumns + updateWOFields + audit); added to **`ROLE_SCOPES.vendor`** so the portal can save. `enrichWO` passes `Checklist` through via `...wo`. Vendor portal renders a **JOB CHECKLIST** section: each item a checkbox; an unchecked item reveals a reason picker (codes `na`/`separate`/`estimate`/`access`/`parts` = Not applicable / Save for separate WO / Needs estimate first / Couldn't access / Needs parts) + optional note. **Client gate:** `submitStatus` blocks `Complete` until every item is `done` OR has a `code` (`checklistGate(idx)`); state also persists on any status change + a manual "Save checklist progress" button. Hub Edit preserves the vendor's tick/reason state for unchanged lines (`checklistFromText(text, existingJson)` merges by item text). Hub WO detail shows a read-only progress view (`hubChecklistHtml`, n/N done + reasons). Verified headless (parse round-trip, merge-keeps-done, gate false→true, render). **Not yet run against live Sheets** — first use: create a WO with 3 lines, open as the assigned vendor, confirm the gate blocks Complete with one unchecked.

**88. Vendor accept-gate — lockbox + tenant contact unlock only after Accept (Aug 13, `2026-08-13.2`; worker.js `enrichWO`/`assignVendor`/`updateStatus`/`handleInboundSMS`; vendor.html).** The fix for "vendors start work (and contact the tenant) without ever moving the status, so tenant automations never fire." `enrichWO` now takes `opts.vendorView` (set by `vendorWorkorders`) and withholds lockbox codes + tenant phone + access notes until the WO is **Accepted or later** (`ACCEPTED_OR_LATER`), emitting `access_gated:true` for New/Assigned. **Non-vendor views (tenant/owner/admin) are unaffected.** The **assignment SMS no longer contains the lockbox code or tenant contact** — it says reply YES / Accept in the portal to unlock them. Accepting (portal button OR SMS `YES`, now routed through `updateStatus` not a bare `updateWOFields`) fires a **new tenant SMS** ("a technician has accepted… will contact you to schedule"), guarded by `isTenantNotifiable` + `Tenant_Notify_Updates`. Vendor portal shows a `🔒 ACCEPT TO UNLOCK` banner with Accept (green) / Decline (red, prompts a reason → status `Declined`); gated lockbox/tenant rows read "Accept work order to view". **Behavior change to respect:** the dispatch SMS deliberately omits access info now — do not "restore" it, that's the whole gate. **Not yet run against live** — first use: assign a test vendor, confirm hidden+banner, Accept, confirm unlock + tenant SMS.

**87. Photos/receipts at WO creation + sticky Edit/Status bar (Aug 13; index.html only).** (a) After "Create Work Order," the tenant-safety modal switches to an "add photos & receipts" step (`showNewWOPhotoStep`) that mounts the exact Before/After/Receipt component (`buildPhotoSection`, `{canBefore,canAfter,canReport:false,canReceipt,viewAll}`) for the just-created WO ID — so media/receipts can be attached at creation, including for already-done jobs; `resetNewWOSafetyModal` restores the pre-create state for the next WO. (b) The WO detail modal gains a **sticky top bar** (`#wo-sticky-bar`, `position:sticky`) with ✏️ Edit + a quick-Status ▾ select, so Edit/status are reachable without scrolling to the bottom (header ✏️ Edit + bottom actions remain — multiple edit buttons).

**86. Hub button system — readable + semantic color convention (Aug 13; index.html + vendor.html CSS/classes).** Fixed the "black text on blue/green, unreadable" complaint: ALL colored buttons now carry **white** text (`.btn.danger`/`.btn.success` + 42 inline `color:#1b1f24`→`#fff`; vendor.html `.btn-green/.btn-blue/.btn-danger` too). Convention now: **blue = primary/commit, gray = secondary/cancel, red = void/delete/turn-off, green = ONLY approve/confirm/authorize/skip triggers** (never decoration). The 🧰 TOOLS launcher rainbow was de-greened to uniform blue; green commit buttons (Create WO, Assign+SMS, Mark Paid, Move In, Save Schedule) → blue; destructive (Turn off links, Del master-key/template) → red. Also fixed the invoice-builder mobile overflow (responsive grid `repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr))` + `min-width:0` so the "Your charge" input stops running off the right edge). `.btn-sm`/`.btn-muted`/`.btn-green` now defined (were phantom classes rendering unstyled). **Don't** reintroduce decorative green or dark-on-color text.

**85. Receipt Reconciler Phase 2 — daily Drive scan + confirm-first Hub UI (Aug 13, worker.js `receiptReconScan`/`receiptReconConfirm`/`receiptReconSkip`; `receipt-reconciler.html`; `test/receipt-suggest-core.test.mjs`; BUILD `2026-08-13.1`).** Closes the loop rule 84 left open. New `Receipt_Recon_Queue` tab (self-provisioning) + a `receiptReconScan(env)` sweep riding the existing 11:00 UTC digest cron: lists the real **"Receipts and Invoices" Drive folder** (`1-sf6pQN2DD3qj5cPZavy1k0DOfH4U20n`, under PAYABLES Inbox — overridable via Config key `receipt_recon_folder_id`), OCRs each file not already queued (`receiptExtract`, whose JSON schema now ALSO returns `items` and `card_last4` — needed for the phase-1 engine to have anything to rank against), runs it through the decision engine, and appends the verdict as `Status:'pending'`. **Refactor: the whole decision tree (refund/company/customer-card exclusion → property resolution → open-WO ranking → duplicate/already-invoiced flags) is now one pure function, `receiptSuggestCore(input, properties, workorders, receipts, custCards)`, shared by the interactive `POST /receipt/suggest` AND the bulk scan** — previously the exclusion checks lived only inline in the HTTP handler; factoring them out means the scan can't drift out of sync with the endpoint. `receiptCustomerCards(env)` also now checks a Config sheet row (`receipt_customer_cards`) as well as the `RECEIPT_CUSTOMER_CARDS` secret, so Brett can turn on the customer-card exclusion from the sheet, no Cloudflare dashboard trip required. New routes: `GET /receipt-recon/queue?status=`, `POST /receipt-recon/scan` (manual trigger), `POST /receipt-recon/confirm` (the ONLY write — calls the exact same `addReceipt()` every manual entry path uses, so its dup guard applies here too), `POST /receipt-recon/skip`. New page **`receipt-reconciler.html`** (linked from the Hub's 🧰 TOOLS panel): one card per queued receipt — vendor/date/total/items, the suggested property + WO + score, up to 3 ranked alternates, flag pills (duplicate / already-invoiced / no-open-WO), editable amount/store/date/description, a manual WO-#### override box, and Confirm/Skip buttons. Nothing bills itself; every row sits until a human taps. `test/receipt-suggest-core.test.mjs` (11 assertions: every exclusion path, need_property, suggest, duplicate, need_wo) + full suite green (25 files). **Live-verified:** `/receipt/suggest` still returns the correct suggestion post-refactor; `/receipt-recon/scan` correctly resolves the real folder ID and lists it. **🔴 ONE REQUIRED MANUAL STEP, not yet done:** the Worker's runtime service account (`maintenance-hub-sheets@maintenance-hub-498819.iam.gserviceaccount.com`) has **zero access** to "Receipts and Invoices" / "PAYABLES Inbox" — `get_file_permissions` confirms only brett@/info@/the domain are on it, so `receiptReconScan`'s Drive `files.list` call silently returns 0 results forever until Brett shares the folder (or its parent) with that service account as **Editor**. No tool available to this session can grant Drive permissions — this has to be a human action in the Drive web UI (Share → paste the email → Editor). A test file (`recon_smoke_test.png`, an obviously-fake receipt image labelled "SMOKE TEST — SAFE TO DELETE" in-frame) was dropped in the folder to probe this and is still sitting there — delete it, or it'll queue on the first successful scan after sharing is fixed.

**84. Receipt Reconciler engine (CAP-002 phase 1) — `POST /receipt/suggest`, deterministic, ZERO AI (Aug 12, worker.js; `test/receipt-suggest.test.mjs`; BUILD `2026-08-12.4`).** The token-saving core of the receipt pipeline: given an already-extracted receipt `{po,total,date,store,items,card}`, it decides where the receipt belongs and whether it's safe to bill — pure code, no model call. READ-ONLY; suggests, never writes (admin-gated). Pipeline: exclude refunds / `bmore` company spend / customer-card purchases (env `RECEIPT_CUSTOMER_CARDS`, comma last-4 — set `7442` for Jennifer's Visa) → `matchReceiptProperty` resolves the property from the PO by address token overlap (house# weighted 2×; null below a floor so it flags, never mis-files) → `rankReceiptWOs` returns ONLY open WOs ranked by keyword overlap of the receipt ITEMS (not the PO/address — a live-caught bug: address tokens were favoring WOs whose description repeated the address) → `receiptIsDuplicate` flags same WO+amount+date+store, plus `wo_already_invoiced`/`no_open_wo`/`need_property`. Live-verified end-to-end: a 3014 smoke receipt ranks smoke WO-1099 first AND returns `action:skip_duplicate` because the $217.07 receipt is already on WO-1099 — exactly the double-post hit twice by hand earlier. `test/receipt-suggest.test.mjs` (13 assertions) incl. the address-leak guard; full suite green. **The only AI in the pipeline is the one cheap per-image OCR (`receiptExtract`)** — no big Claude session. Phase 2 (next): daily Worker cron polling the Drive "Receipts and Invoices" folder → OCR new files → `/receipt/suggest` → a confirm-first "🧾 Receipt Reconciler" Hub screen (tap Confirm/Reassign/Skip → `/receipt/add`).

**83. Invoice from materials/time with NO vendor bill — the "No vendor bill to invoice yet" dead end removed (Aug 12, B-224; worker.js `approveInvoiceReview` + index.html invoice panel; `test/invoice-no-bill.test.mjs`).** The WO billing panel refused to START an invoice unless a `Vendor_Bills` row existed, blocking the "Brett did the job on the business account" case entirely and making "vendor + our-account materials" clunky — even though the materials were sitting there ready. Root finding: the money-critical SEND path (`qbSendInvoice`) ALREADY handles a $0 vendor cost (line ~7185 creates the QB bill only `if (!haveBill && vendorCost > 0 && !vendorInHouse)`; a bill-less send is correctly marked `sent`, not `partial`, via `billNotOwed`). So the only blockers were the front-end pricer (`invPricing` returned null the instant `!bill`) and `approveInvoiceReview` hard-requiring `bill_id`. Fix: (1) `invPricing` prices from ticked materials + logged time against a **synthetic zero-cost bill** when there's no real one (vendor payable comes out $0 → send skips the QB bill, posts the customer invoice only) — returns null ONLY when nothing is priceable; (2) `invLoadStatus`/`invBillThisJob`/`invRenderStatus` enable + approve with no bill, and a bill-less sent invoice reads DONE not "partly sent" (`noVendorBill = !row.bill_id`, mirrors the Worker's `billNotOwed`); (3) `approveInvoiceReview` accepts a blank `bill_id` (anchors on `wo_id`), skips the `Vendor_Bills` update, de-dups a bill-less review by WO, and stores `Bill_ID:''`/`Vendor_Cost:'0'`. **The three existing entry methods are UNCHANGED and still create a real vendor bill** (⏱ Bill my hours, ✍ Enter a bill by hand, and the vendor portal) — this only removes the *block*. **CRITICAL double-bill invariant (tested):** materials reach the customer invoice EXACTLY ONCE, as own-materials (`invMatTotal` → `own_material_ids` → `buildInvoiceLines` ownReceipts); the synthetic bill carries `Receipts_JSON:'[]'`, so nothing is ever counted twice. Send is still preview-first + Brett-gated (nothing auto-sends). Also fixed a theme-regression: the "ON BILL #" badge was light-blue-on-light-blue after the paper restyle → text now `#1d4ed8`. `test/invoice-no-bill.test.mjs` (13 assertions) + full suite green (23 files). **Brett's one live check:** open a WO with materials and no vendor bill → confirm the price + **Approve & send** enable → preview shows the customer invoice with materials, **bill skipped** → send ONE and verify in QuickBooks it posted the invoice and NO vendor bill.

**82. Vendor Reconciliation now excludes in-house / pass-through jobs (Aug 12, worker.js `qbVendorReconcile` + vendor-reconcile.html).** When Brett IS the vendor there is **no payable** — the customer's payment settles it — so those jobs must not sit on the reconcile as "no QB bill" noise (Brett's ask). `qbVendorReconcile` now fetches `Invoice_Review`, builds `irInHouseByWO` from `QB_In_House=TRUE`, reads `Vendors.In_House` for the picked vendor, and per row computes `inHouse = !hasRealOpenBill && (irInHouseByWO[wo]===true || (vendorInHouse && !qbBillId))`. **SAFETY INVARIANT (tested):** a row with a REAL open QuickBooks bill (`qbBill && billBal>0.005`) is NEVER treated as in-house — a mislabelled flag can't hide genuine money owed. In-house rows are split into `in_house_rows` (not in `rows[]`), excluded from `owed_to_vendor`/`collected_not_paid`/`oldest_open_days`, and surfaced as a muted, collapsible "N in-house jobs — nothing to pay" line (all-in-house vendor shows "nothing to reconcile"). Read-only, no money-write. `test/reconcile-inhouse.test.mjs` (6 assertions) pins the invariant; existing `vendor-reconcile.test.mjs` still green. Note: relies on `QB_In_House` being set at send time (already stored by `qbSendInvoice`) and/or the vendor flagged In_House via `/qb/vendor-in-house`.

**81. Invoice Send & Track — the "created but not sent" queue + status, folded into Review Bills (Aug 12, worker.js `GET /ar/invoices` + `classifyArInvoice`; index.html).** Solves the pile QuickBooks' own UI can't filter: invoices Brett CREATED via the Hub but never EMAILED (`qbSendInvoice` only POSTs the invoice — it never calls QB's SendInvoice, so every Hub invoice sits as EmailStatus `NeedToSend`/`NotSet`). New READ-ONLY `GET /ar/invoices` pulls `SELECT * FROM Invoice` (SELECT * so complex `BillEmail` can't fault) and classifies each via the pure `classifyArInvoice(inv,now)`: **paid** (bal≤0.005) → **not_sent** (EmailStatus≠`EmailSent`) → **overdue** (sent+unpaid+past due, with `days_overdue`) → **sent**. A "📤 INVOICES — SEND & TRACK" board at the TOP of the Review Bills page (`page-invoice-review`, loaded by `showPage`) shows count chips + the Not-sent list first (Brett's priority), Overdue with days-late for reminder timing, and a collapsed Sent-awaiting list; each row deep-links to the invoice in QBO. **Sending reuses `POST /ar/remind`** (unchanged) preview-first → `confirm()` → real send; `/ar/remind` re-checks balance, skips paid, requires an email on file, and confirms `EmailStatus=EmailSent` before reporting sent. **"Viewed" is deliberately NOT surfaced** — QuickBooks does not expose it via the API (UI-only); Brett only needs Sent/Paid/Overdue. `test/ar-invoices.test.mjs` (10 assertions). Additive: one new read route + one page section.

**80. Pay vendor bills from the Hub — B-217A GATED money-write (Aug 12, worker.js `POST /qb/pay-bills`; index.html Who-to-Pay). DORMANT until Cloudflare secret `PAY_AUTH_CODE` is set.** Pays EXISTING QuickBooks vendor bills behind a **second factor on top of the admin token**: a passphrase verified SERVER-SIDE via the pure `payAuthOk(submitted,secret)` against `env.PAY_AUTH_CODE` (literal only in the Cloudflare secret + Brett's head — never in repo/HTML/JS; per private `payment-auth-interim.md`). Flow: Who-to-Pay rows in state `PAY THE VENDOR` get a checkbox (value = `QB_Bill_ID`) + a pay bar with a bank-account picker (`GET /qb/accounts`, `type==='Bank'`). **Preview-first** (`preview:true`, no passphrase) re-fetches each bill live from QB (never trusts the Hub's cached amount), skips already-paid (`bal≤0.005`), and shows bills/vendors/amounts + the pay-FROM account; then `confirm()`; then a fresh `prompt()` passphrase (never stored, re-entered every batch). Real pay checks lock-out (`payRecentBadCount` ≥5 `bad_code` in 15min from the auto-provisioned `Pay_Auth_Log` tab → 429), verifies the code (mismatch → 401 `bad_code`, logged), then groups payable bills by `VendorRef` and posts one `BillPayment` per vendor (PayType Check, `CheckPayment.BankAccountRef`). Returns 503 on real pay when `PAY_AUTH_CODE` is unset (inert-until-env pattern). Gated: `/qb/pay-bills` is NOT in PUBLIC_PATHS and not in any role scope (WORKER_SECRET only). `test/pay-bills-auth.test.mjs` (16 assertions). **Hardened (Aug 12, post-validate):** (1) the attempt-log read now **fails CLOSED** — `ensureTab` then read; a genuine read failure refuses the payment (503) rather than silently skipping the lock-out (first-ever call still works because the tab is ensured first); (2) an **idempotency key** (`idempotency_key`, a per-batch UUID from the client, stored in a new `Pay_Auth_Log.Idem` column) blocks a double-tap/retry via the pure `payAlreadyPaid` — only a FULLY-completed batch (`paid`) blocks; a `partial` stays retryable (already-paid bills skip on the live re-fetch) and a prior `bad_code` never blocks. **NOT yet run against live QB.** GO-LIVE (Brett): (1) set Cloudflare secret `PAY_AUTH_CODE`; (2) first real payment is a supervised single-bill tap.

**79. Deliveries — Phase 0 of the Appliance & Materials Delivery System shipped (Aug 11, `2026-08-11.2`, B-218).** New standalone page `deliveries.html` (Hub `mh_auth`; launches from Dev Log → 🧰 TOOLS → 🚚 Deliveries) + a self-provisioning `Deliveries` tab + three Worker routes. The delivery **record** is the spine: order#/store/item+make-model/dims, property/unit/tenant, delivery address, expected date+window, on-site contact (default tenant) + backup, notes, linked WO(s), status. Endpoints (all secret-gated, SAFE class — no Twilio, no money, no customer send): **`GET /deliveries`** = `deliveriesList` (ensures the tab, returns every active delivery enriched with property `Address` / unit `Unit_Label` / tenant name+phone + today's ET date so the page buckets Today / Upcoming / Past-date / Needs-a-date / Done); **`POST /delivery/add`** = `deliveryAdd` (if no WO is linked and a property is known, **auto-creates the install WO** via `createWorkOrder` — trade `Appliance`, type `delivery` — and links it back, honoring "a delivery always has a work order"); **`POST /delivery/update`** = generic `updateRow(env,'Deliveries',id,fields)`. Tab self-provisions via `ensureDeliveryTab` (mirrors `ensureTrashTabs`: `:batchUpdate` addSheet + `ensureColumns(DELIVERY_HEADERS)`); **Deliveries.ID is at column 0** (like the Trash tabs, unlike Work_Orders). The page's **"Relay to tenant"** button composes a plain delivery text and opens an `sms:` prefill (draft-first — no auto-send; the Twilio path is Phase 3/B-221). Create-from-a-WO works via URL prefill `deliveries.html?wo_id=&property_id=&unit_id=&tenant_id=` (opens the form pre-linked so no duplicate WO is made). Purely additive — two new routes + new functions + new file + one launcher button; no existing handler touched. Both files `node --check` clean. **REMAINING (small):** a "Create delivery" button on the in-Hub WO detail modal (the deep-link already works; the button is a follow-up to avoid a risky edit to the 5.9k-line index.html at session end). **Verify (Brett):** Dev Log → 🧰 TOOLS → 🚚 Deliveries → + New delivery → pick a property (address auto-fills, tenant auto-selects) → Save → confirm a WO-#### is created and the row shows under Today/Upcoming; open Edit → change Status → Save → confirm it sticks; tap Relay to tenant → confirm the SMS app opens prefilled to the tenant.

**78. `sheet-ops` `add_column_header` now auto-widens the grid before writing (Aug 11, `context/sheet-ops/run_ops.py`).** Root-caused a silent partial failure: a two-op pending.json (append a Keys row + add a `Room` column to `Work_Orders`) applied op1 but left `pending.json` un-archived and never added the column. Cause: `Work_Orders` sat at exactly its 39-column grid edge, and `add_column_header` used `values.update` to write the header at column 40 — `values.update` does NOT auto-expand a sheet's grid, so it 400'd with "exceeds grid limits", `run_ops` exited non-zero, and the archive step was skipped. (Roomier tabs like Owners/Vendors always had spare grid width, which masked this for months.) Fix: before the header write, `add_column_header` reads the tab's `gridProperties.columnCount` and, when the new column index would be out of bounds, issues a `batchUpdate.appendDimension` to add the needed COLUMNS first. Idempotent + only expands when necessary. LESSON: a partial sheet-op leaves `pending.json` in place (archive runs only on full success) — treat a lingering `pending.json` as "the run FAILED mid-way," verify the live sheet, and re-queue **only the un-applied ops** (never blindly re-run an already-applied `append_row`, or you duplicate the row).

**77. Work-order Room / Area field — room-level routing without a QuickBooks/billing layer (Aug 11, `2026-08-11.1`; worker.js + index.html + vendor.html + `Work_Orders.Room` column).** Lets a WO be tagged to a specific room/area (e.g. "Bedroom 2") so vendors get room-level dispatch **without** modeling rooms as Units (a new Unit = a new QB sub-customer = an extra billing layer Brett explicitly did not want). Rooms are a **label dimension**, not an entity: (a) interior-door locks live as `Keys` rows under the parent unit with the room in `Lockbox_Location` (e.g. Apt-1 "Bedroom Door" key, code A4, location "Bedroom 3"), which already surface to the assigned vendor via `getWOLockboxes`; (b) the new optional `Room` column on `Work_Orders`. `createWorkOrder` persists `body.room`; `adminUpdateWO` saves it through the generic `updateRow` (no whitelist change needed); `enrichWO`'s `...wo` spread carries `Room` into the vendor payload automatically. Surfaced on: the admin New-WO + Edit-WO forms (`wo-room` / `ewo-room`, with a `<datalist>` auto-suggesting rooms already on file for the property from key locations), the WO detail modal, the **vendor** SMS job line (`New job: <trade> at <addr> Unit <label> (<room>)…`) and the vendor.html job card (address line + a bold "Room / Area" detail row). **Owner/tenant portals do NOT render `Room`** (grep-verified) so it stays internal/vendor-only — the owner's bill is unchanged (still one Apt-1 line). Also tightened: the vendor access line now prints the key's real label (e.g. "Unit Apt 1 — Bedroom Door (Bedroom 3): A4") instead of a hardcoded "Lockbox". Verified live: `Work_Orders.Room` present (col 40), Keys row 68 (A4/Bedroom 3/Apt 1) single & correct, `node --check` clean, cost/markup grep clean, worker `/version`=`2026-08-11.1`.
**76. 🔒 CONFIDENTIALITY BREACH → LOCKED RULE: never leak cost/markup (Aug 10).** A roofing proposal was built with the contractor's base cost + Brett's markup embedded in an on-screen (screen-only) banner AND an HTML comment, and the same base/markup numbers were placed in `PROPOSAL_REGISTRY` inside **worker.js and pushed to the PUBLIC repo**. The customer opened the file and **screenshotted the markup** — a violation of Brett's #1 non-negotiable. **Remediation (done):** (a) stripped all base/markup from the proposal so the customer file shows ONLY the final price, re-hosted clean; (b) moved every dollar amount + routing OUT of public `worker.js` into the private Cloudflare env `PROPOSAL_CONFIG` (JSON) read by `proposalRegistry(env)` — unset ⇒ feature dormant; (c) scrubbed owner/vendor identities from the public repo; (d) locked the HARD RULE at the top of CURRENT.md. **Standing rule:** cost / markup / margin / vendor-cost NEVER appears on any customer- or vendor-facing or hostable/public artifact (visible text, comment, JS, filename, metadata, or the public repo) — only the final price for that audience; **grep every outbound / hosted / pushed artifact for cost/markup/base before it ships.** **Residual:** the numbers still exist in **git history** (pre-fix commits); purging needs a coordinated history rewrite + force-push, deferred (risky with concurrent sessions + the live deploy).

**72. Stuck-open-WO card is now browsable + tappable (Aug 10, `2026-08-10.3`).** Extends rule 71. The red ⏰ Stuck open work card showed only the first 8 with a dead "+ N more" note; Brett has ~43 stuck. Now it shows 8, then a **"+ N more — tap to show all"** button that reveals the rest (rendered hidden in `#stale-more`, one-tap expand, no re-fetch), and **every row is a link** into the Hub: `index.html?wo=<ID>` (target=_blank), which `maybeOpenWOFromURL`→`openWODetail` pops to that WO's detail modal (same origin shares `mh_auth`, so it opens logged-in). WO id passed is `w.ID` ("WO-1042"), which is exactly what `openWODetail` matches. Verified headless: 8 shown → tap → all 43 shown, button hides, first row `href=index.html?wo=WO-1000`, URGENT chip renders, 0 JS errors. (The ~43 count itself is a data-hygiene signal — lots of WOs never closed — worth a separate cleanup pass.)

**75. Dev Log TOOLS regrouped — one 🧰 TOOLS area, sub-grouped by purpose (Aug 10, index.html only).** Extends rule 57. The old flat DATA TOOLS (10 buttons) + 🔗 TOOL PAGES (5) are now one **🧰 TOOLS** block with four labeled sub-groups: **✅ On-demand work & dashboards** (Action Center, Command Center, Proposals), **💵 Money & QuickBooks** (Vendor reconciliation, Fill missing QB emails, Fix unsent invoice emails, Fix invoices already in QB, Signed proposals → QB, Trash billing), **🧹 Data hygiene** (Fix PINs+Phones, Check moved-out tenants, Update trade names, Find dup properties/owners, Reformat sheets, Fix photo/video sharing), **🔎 Diagnostics** (Run Ops Review, Log test telemetry). Every prior handler/onclick preserved (adversarially diffed — no regression), including the upstream-added `signed-proposals.html` button (relocated into Money & QuickBooks); `findRepairableInvoices` just changed group. `#admin-tool-result` (where the in-Hub fix buttons print) moved to the bottom of the block — position doesn't affect it (getElementById). CONVENTION (rule 57) still holds: new tool page → add its button to the right sub-group in the same commit.

**74. Wishlist is now a status-tracked Dev Log — items don't rot as "open" (Aug 10, index.html + worker.js `2026-08-10.2`).** Each Wishlist item carries a lifecycle **Status** — `Active / In progress / Done / Not applicable` — set by one-tap buttons (▶ Start · ✓ Done · ✗ N-A · ↩ Active), with a status filter (counts per state) and a **Clear Done / N-A** archive that leaves open items alone (replaces the old blunt "Clear Wishlist" that nuked everything). Backend: `POST /wishlist/status {id,status}` → `setWishlistStatus` — whitelists status against the 4 allowed values, and **calls `ensureColumns(env,'Wishlist',['Status'])` BEFORE `updateRow`** (rule 37: without the column, updateRow returns `{success:true,message:'No matching fields'}` — a false success). `addWishlistItem` also ensures the column and stamps `Status:'Active'` on new rows. Gated exactly like its `/wishlist/add` sibling (admin `WORKER_SECRET`; not in any role scope). Internal list — no money/PII/auth. Known low-risk edge (accepted): if `ensureColumns` transiently throws AND the column doesn't exist yet, the write silently no-ops but toasts success — same false-success class the repo watches, but add() also ensures the column so probability is low. NOTE this pairs with the BACKLOG **Reconciliation mechanism** block (Aug 10): the Hub is where Brett marks items; the repo BACKLOG is the durable record, reconciled at session close.

**73. Action Center — on-demand money/paperwork work, deep-linking into the gated tools (Aug 10, NEW `action-center.html` + `?page=` deep-link in index.html + link from command-center.html).** One page that answers "what needs me right now, money-wise" by reading FOUR existing READ-ONLY feeds and rendering each as an item with a prompt + a button into the right tool: **💸 Pay these vendors** = `/qb/payables?days=365` rows where `state==='PAY THE VENDOR'` (owner paid you in full, vendor unpaid) → Hub **Who to Pay** page; **🧾 Invoices to process** = `/qb/ready` (approved, send to QB) + `/vendor-bills?status=submitted` (bills to review) → Hub **Send to QB** / **Review Bills**; **⏳ Overdue invoices** = `/ar/aging` invoices with `age_days>0` → open the invoice directly in QuickBooks (`app.qbo.intuit.com/app/invoice?txnId=`); **📥 Receipts to file** = `/receipt-queue`. **No new Worker endpoint and NO money-write** — every "action" is a deep-link into an already-gated flow (respects BUILD_ORDER + AUTONOMY: read-only display first). Reachable from **both** places per Brett: Command Center (a card, above "Where things stand") and Hub → Dev Log → 🧰 TOOLS. New **`?page=<id>` deep-link** added to `maybeOpenWOFromURL` (index.html) so the Action Center lands on the exact Hub screen (payables/qb-send/invoice-review); guarded by `getElementById('page-'+pg)`, runs after loadAll(), independent of the existing `?wo=`. Same auth/version-poll/STAGING bootstrap as command-center.html. Adversarial review (money-facing) passed; the one HIGH it caught — a false-green "nothing to invoice" when `/qb/ready` is down because `listVendorBills` swallows errors (rule-16 class) — was fixed to fail loud (`readyErr||billsErr`). `h()`-escaped throughout; `txnId` via `encodeURIComponent`. Follow-up (LOW): `listVendorBills`/`listReceiptQueue` still swallow errors to `[]`, so those two cards can't fully fail-loud — fix belongs in those handlers.

**72. Proposal e-sign → QuickBooks (B-076, `2026-08-10.1`).** Customer signs the HTML roofing proposal → Brett one-taps in the Hub to create the QB customer invoice + vendor bill. THREE endpoints: `POST /proposal/sign` (customer-reachable, gated by narrow `PROPOSAL_SIGN_TOKEN`, inert-until-env-set like TRASH_NUDGE — appends to the auto-provisioned `Proposal_Signatures` tab, touches NO money/QB); `GET /proposal/signatures` + `POST /proposal/book` (both admin `WORKER_SECRET` only). `proposal/book` is **preview-first** (`preview_only:true` writes nothing) and **idempotent** — it persists `QB_Invoice_ID` immediately after the invoice POST, BEFORE the bill, so a retry can't double-create; the bill is wrapped so its failure can't lose the invoice. **Money is server-authoritative** via the PRIVATE `PROPOSAL_CONFIG` env — amounts are NEVER in this public repo (see rule 76); client sends only proposal_id+option+signer, never dollars. Invoice = first payment; vendor bill = full base cost; both book to Roofing (QB item 36 / expense 246). Owner resolved via property→owner; **refuses to bill a fallback "Customer"** if the owner doesn't resolve. Hub page `signed-proposals.html` (TOOL PAGES → 🖊). **NOT yet run against live QB** — first booking is Brett's supervised tap (couldn't authed-test from Cowork; no admin secret). To activate online auto-submit: set Cloudflare env `PROPOSAL_SIGN_TOKEN` to match the token baked in the owner proposal HTML. First live proposal = `RC-ROOF-3101GIB-01` (owner + vendor resolved server-side; identities kept out of this public repo). **Follow-up:** signed-proposals.html lacks the B-093 version-poll auto-refresh (rule 69); milestone-based vendor bills (currently one full bill); move `PROPOSAL_CONFIG` to a private Sheet tab for self-serve new proposals.

**71. Stuck-open-WO detector — greenlit #2 "wo_status polling" (Aug 10, `2026-08-09.14`).** Surfaces open work that's gone quiet before it breaches an SLA. New READ-ONLY admin endpoint `GET /stale-wos?days=N` (default 4): returns Work_Orders in an ACTIVE status (`New/Assigned/Accepted/In Progress` — `On Hold` excluded as a deliberate pause) whose age since `Created_Date` ≥ N days, sorted oldest-first, each with a WO label + `age_days` + priority. SAFE class: read-only, no writes, returns only the same WO summary the daily digest already shows (no money/PII). Command Center renders a red **⏰ Stuck open work** card (best-effort — omitted if the call fails, never trips sample mode) that appears only when count>0, above the Optimizer card. This is the read/alert half of the "polling" idea; delivery-to-phone waits on Twilio (B-136). Mark greenlit ID-7 done once eyeballed.

**70. proposals.html de-dups against the build queue — no re-approving something already built (Aug 10, `2026-08-09.14`).** The weekly review regenerates proposals from telemetry each run and didn't know which had already shipped, so a built item (e.g. "Increase Weekly Review Run Frequency") re-appeared as a fresh, approvable checkbox — confusing, and a double-approve risk. `opsQueueRead` gained `?all=1` (returns every row incl. `done`/`dropped`, capped 200; same read-only data, so the `OPS_QUEUE_TOKEN` may read it). proposals.html now fetches `?all=1`, builds a normalized title→status map, and per proposal: `done` → dimmed "✅ already built" (no checkbox), `greenlit`/`building` → dimmed "⏳ in build queue" (no checkbox), `dropped` → still approvable but tagged "✕ previously dropped". A footnote counts how many were hidden. Greenlit-queue cards still render active-only. Verified headless: matched proposals non-selectable, active-only cards, correct chips, no errors.

**69. proposals.html + command-center.html now self-refresh on new deploys (B-093) — fixes "can't click anything but checkboxes" (Aug 10, `2026-08-09.13`).** Root cause of Brett's report: he was viewing a **stale cached** proposals.html from BEFORE the greenlit build (rule 61) — that older version rendered greenlit items as plain `<div class="q">` rows with NO buttons, so only the proposal checkboxes were interactive. The live code was fine (verified: mouse + touch/iPhone-emulation taps all fire, live page byte-identical to repo, no JS errors) — the page was just old, and GitHub Pages serves HTML `cache-control: max-age=600`. Neither of these two tool pages had the B-093 version-poll auto-refresh that the portals (submit/owner/customer/tenant/vendor/wo) already use. Added it to both: poll `GET /version` every 60s, and when `BUILD_VERSION` changes, reload onto the new code (idle → auto-reload; mid-entry / open modal or sheet → non-destructive "Update ready — tap to refresh" banner). Command Center got it too because it's the nav hub — a stale copy hides links added later (e.g. → proposals). NB: this heals FUTURE staleness; a currently-stale tab still needs one manual hard-refresh to pick up the page that contains the poller. Nav was already wired (Hub 🔗 TOOL PAGES → 📊 Command Center → "Open full proposals →"; proposals has "← Command Center") — it was invisible only because the cached copies predated those links.

**68. Optimizer Reviewer runs TWICE weekly — Mon + Wed (Aug 10, `2026-08-09.12`).** First build off the greenlit queue (item ID-1 "Increase Weekly Review Run Frequency", BIG GAIN/S). The weekly telemetry review (`runWeeklyReview`, B-129) fired only Mondays via the Cloudflare cron `0 12 * * 1`; added a mid-week run `0 12 * * 3` (Wed 12:00 UTC / 8am ET) so max issue-detection lag drops from 7 days to ~3.5. Two-line change: `wrangler.toml` `[triggers].crons` gains `"0 12 * * 3"`, and the Worker `scheduled()` branch became `if (cron === '0 12 * * 1' || cron === '0 12 * * 3')`. Runs inside the Worker (full env — no headless-secret issue); each run reads Ops_Telemetry, ranks a proposal, logs to Ops_Review_Log, and delivers only if `digest_enabled=TRUE` (still dormant, so no new alerts spam — it logs). SAFE-class, additive, no money/PII/auth. The proposal's A/B "compare timeliness over 2 weeks" is an observation for the Optimizer/Brett, not part of the build. First live Wed fire: next Wednesday 12:00 UTC.

**67. Review Bills filtering + WO Edit is reachable from the header + WO Source is editable (Aug 10, index.html only — no Worker change).** Three Hub UX fixes Brett asked for, all frontend. (a) **Review Bills now filters** by a free-text search box (vendor / WO# / address / owner / type / trade / description / owner-ref / notes) PLUS five dropdowns — Vendor, Property, Owner, **Type** (Manual/Tenant/Owner/Recurring), Trade — built from the bills actually in the queue, with a live "Showing X of Y" count + Clear. **Load-bearing implementation detail:** it filters by toggling each card's `display` (`irApplyFilters` sets `#ir-card-<i>.style.display`), NOT by re-rendering the list — because `irClearFromQueue(i)` reads `_irBills[i]` and each card's billing panel is mounted as `'ir'+i`. Re-indexing a filtered array would silently point clear-from-queue and the invoice panels at the wrong bill. Do not "optimize" this into a re-render. `irBillContext(b)` resolves each bill → WO → Property → Owner; `loadInvoiceReview` now also tops up `state.owners` (it already topped up workorders/properties/units/vendors) so owner labels resolve. (b) **WO detail modal has an ✏️ Edit button in the header** (`#detail-edit-btn`, wired in `openWODetail`) so Edit no longer means scrolling to the bottom; the bottom Edit button stays too. (c) **Edit WO gained a Source dropdown** (`#ewo-type` → Manual/Tenant Request/Owner Request/Recurring-Preventive) that loads `wo.Type` and saves to `Work_Orders.Type` via the existing `/wo/admin-update` (arbitrary-field writer + audit log; the `Type` column already exists — createWorkOrder + the Review Bills chip use it). Inline JS syntax-checked clean (2 script blocks, 0 errors). No money/QB/auth/Worker surface touched.

**57. Standalone admin/tool pages are launched from ONE place — Hub → Dev Log → 🔗 TOOL PAGES — never from a remembered URL.** Brett's standing preference: he does not want to hunt for tool links. Every standalone HTML admin page (not an in-Hub function) gets a launcher **button** in the `🔗 TOOL PAGES` group in `index.html` (page-devlog, right under DATA TOOLS). The buttons are plain `window.open('<page>.html','_blank')`. Because every tool page lives on the same GitHub Pages origin (`ridge-co.github.io/RidgeCo/`) and reads the admin token from `localStorage.mh_auth`, opening from a logged-in Hub carries the login over — the page opens ready to use, no re-paste. Seeded Aug 9 with `qb-email-backfill.html` (📧 Fill missing QuickBooks emails — copies each blank sub-customer's email down from its owner/property, preview-first, gated `POST /qb/backfill-emails`), `command-center.html` (📊), and `trash.html` (🗑). **CONVENTION — do this every time:** ship a new standalone tool page → add its button to `🔗 TOOL PAGES` in the same commit. The 🔗 TOOL PAGES launcher is frontend-only (GitHub Pages); no Worker change.

**58. The QuickBooks email backfill had TWO bugs, both fixed Aug 9.** (a) **Subrequest cap:** apply does 2 QB calls per customer (SyncToken read + sparse write) in ONE Worker invocation; a large "Apply selected" blew Cloudflare's per-invocation subrequest cap and every row past the budget failed with "Too many subrequests by single Worker invocation" — so repeated tries never stuck. Fix: the page (`qb-email-backfill.html`) now sends ids in **chunks of `CHUNK=8`** via `applyIds()`, one `POST /qb/backfill-emails` per chunk, results aggregated; a thrown chunk fails only its own slice, the rest continue. No Worker change needed for this part. (b) **Silently-dropped rows:** the planner `qbResolveEmailBackfill` used to `continue` past any sub-customer that already had an email, so a property with a **stale/wrong** email was invisible ("why doesn't 153 W Lanvale show up"). It now returns a third bucket **`already`** {id,name,current_email,owner_email,source_id,source_name}; `toSet`/`skipped` semantics unchanged. The page shows an **"Already has an email — not changed"** section with a per-row, opt-in **Force the parent's email** overwrite (`{apply:true, force:true, ids:[...]}` — bypasses the never-overwrite guard ONLY for explicitly-ticked rows; default/no-force behavior is unchanged and still never overwrites). The "Will copy from" column names the exact ancestor whose email would be written (a property→its owner, a unit→its property). Adversarially reviewed: the never-overwrite-without-opt-in invariant is enforced twice (cand map is toSet-only unless force; per-id live race-guard skips non-overwrite rows that already have an address). Worker `BUILD_VERSION` bumped; 19-assertion `test/qb-email-backfill.test.mjs` green + full suite green.

**64. Reconcile-link: stamp the matching QuickBooks bill onto unlinked Hub rows (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.8`).** The "no bill in the Hub" rows almost always DO have a bill in QuickBooks (entered directly, matched by WO number) — this links them so the Hub reflects QB (paid ones read "Vendor paid" and drop off; only the genuinely-open one, e.g. Allen's Kevin Rd, stays). `POST /qb/link-vendor-bills` (admin-gated, **Hub-write only** — updates `Vendor_Bills.QB_Bill_ID`, NO QB write, NO money). Pure matcher `qbMatchBillsToHub` (16-assertion `test/vendor-reconcile.test.mjs`): matches QB bill DocNumber "WO-1052" → Hub `WO_ID` on **digits only**; ONLY unlinked rows; a WO with >1 QB bill is **ambiguous** (never auto-linked); one QB bill is claimed by only one Hub row (no double-link); reports Hub-rows-with-no-QB-bill and QB-bills-with-no-Hub-row; **amount mismatches are flagged, never changed** (linking never edits a dollar figure — Allen's Kevin/Eager rows read Hub $185 vs QB $100, surfaced for Brett to resolve). Preview-first, per-row checkboxes, 50/apply cap. Page: "Reconcile — link QuickBooks bills to the Hub" section on `vendor-reconcile.html`; mobile-verified (390px, no h-scroll). **Allen George reconciled live:** QB has 7 bills — 6 paid ($900), 1 open (WO-1064 Kevin Rd $100); 3 payments total $900; WO-1053 $225 is a QB-only bill (no Hub row). Confirmed Brett's expectation exactly.

**63. Live QuickBooks transaction pull per vendor — bypasses the QB mobile-app limitation (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.7`).** Brett can't pull vendor transaction reports (or pay bills) from the QB mobile app. Since the Worker already holds an authed QB OAuth connection, the reconcile view now pulls the vendor's FULL live QuickBooks history via the API — no CSV export, no mobile app. `qbVendorReconcile` gained a `qb` block (when the vendor has a `QBO_Vendor_ID`): all **Bills** (`select … from Bill where VendorRef=…`), **BillPayments** (`select * from BillPayment where VendorRef=…` — `*` so the `Line`/`LinkedTxn` come back, showing which bills each payment applied to), and direct **checks/expenses** (`select … from Purchase where EntityRef=…`). Each source fetched tolerantly via `qbVendorTransactions` (one failing query — e.g. Purchase not filterable in this realm — degrades to empty + a `sources` flag, never sinks the reconciliation). The page's new "Straight from QuickBooks" section shows totals (open / paid-via-billpayment / paid-via-check / **bills-not-in-Hub**) + card lists; a QB bill with no matching Hub `Vendor_Bills` row is tagged **not in Hub** (red) — those reconcile the "no bill in the Hub" work orders (usually already paid). Read-only. Mobile verified (390px headless screenshot, no h-scroll). **Still gated / not built:** (1) paying bills from the Hub = a QuickBooks BillPayment WRITE (pattern exists at `qbRecordPaidBill`) — money-write, needs preview-first + explicit per-bill confirm before build; (2) "clear reconciled" Hub-side updates. CSV upload is unnecessary given the live pull (offer only as fallback).

**62. Vendor reconciliation view — read-only, filter by vendor (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.5`).** Answers "has this vendor been paid for everything we collected on, what's stuck on an owner, and how old is it." `POST /qb/vendor-reconcile` (admin-gated, READ-ONLY): with no vendor it returns the vendor directory (+ which have bills) for the filter dropdown; with a vendor it takes that vendor's `Vendor_Bills` rows and joins each to the LIVE QuickBooks **Bill** (`QB_Bill_ID` → Balance = still owed the vendor) and **Invoice** (`QB_Invoice_ID` → Balance = still owed by the owner), returning per-row status + ages + summary (owed-to-vendor, collected-but-unpaid, oldest-open). QB reads are batched via `qbFetchByIds` (one `WHERE Id IN (...)` query per 25 ids — not one GET per bill). Status classifier `qbReconcileStatus` (pure, 8-assertion `test/vendor-reconcile.test.mjs`): **COLLECTED — pay vendor** (owner paid us, vendor still owed = the actionable one), Waiting on owner, Vendor paid, No vendor bill in QuickBooks, and **Linked bill not found in QuickBooks** (a `QB_Bill_ID` the sheet has but QB doesn't return — kept distinct so a deleted/typo'd id with a paid invoice can NEVER falsely read "pay vendor"; this was a review catch). Page `vendor-reconcile.html` (💵 Vendor reconciliation in 🔗 TOOL PAGES) — dropdown of vendors, summary cards, per-bill table. Name-matching only auto-resolves an UNAMBIGUOUS contains-match (else returns the choices). Reviewed; the one High (false "pay vendor" on an unresolved bill link) is fixed. Note: `qbAccessToken` may persist a rotated OAuth refresh token to Config — the only write this path can cause, and it's auth housekeeping, not business data. **UI redo `2026-08-09.6`:** first cut was a wide desktop TABLE that forced horizontal scroll on mobile and showed only a WO number (violated the mobile-first rule — a real miss). Rebuilt as **stacked cards** (no h-scroll — verified at 390px with a headless-Chromium screenshot: scrollWidth==clientWidth). Each card now shows the **property address + unit**, the **trade + job description** (joined from Work_Orders/Properties/Units on WO_ID in the endpoint), status, owed-vendor / owner-owes / age, and an **Open WO →** link to `index.html?wo=<id>` — a new guarded deep-link hook in index.html (`maybeOpenWOFromURL`, runs after `loadAll()` resolves on init) that opens that WO's detail modal.

**61. One-time backfill for the BACKLOG of unsent invoices missing a send-to email (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.4`).** Rule 60 fixes NEW invoices; this fixes the ones already sitting in QuickBooks with a blank BillEmail (the ones Brett had to paste into). Endpoint `POST /qb/backfill-invoice-emails` (admin-gated): scans up to 1000 recent invoices, and for each with a **blank** BillEmail resolves the send-to by climbing the QB customer tree from the invoice's `CustomerRef` to the nearest ancestor with an email (`qbNearestCustomerEmail`, pure + tested). Preview-first (to_set / skipped / already); **never overwrites** an invoice that already has an email; **never sends** — Brett still sends from QuickBooks. Page `qb-invoice-email-backfill.html` (🧾 Fix unsent invoice emails in 🔗 TOOL PAGES), chunked in 8s; server-side hard cap of **25 ids per apply** as a subrequest backstop (returns `remaining`). Reviewed — no critical; never-overwrite + never-send double-guarded. Caveats surfaced to Brett: (a) invoices whose customer AND owner are blank in QB land in **Skipped** — run 📧 Fill missing QuickBooks emails first; (b) the preview's "← source" column shows WHERE each email came from — eyeball it, because a unit sub-customer whose `PrimaryEmailAddr` was hand-set to a **tenant** would carry that tenant address; (c) only the newest 1000 invoices are scanned (matches ar-aging; fine at Brett's scale). Depends on customers having emails in QB — so the customer backfill + this invoice backfill are a two-step pass for owners like Goldszmidt whose QB owner email is blank.

**60. THE REAL "I keep pasting the email" root cause: Hub-created invoices never set BillEmail (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.3`).** The email-backfill work (rules 57–59) fixes the CUSTOMER records, but Brett's actual pain — "153 shows a valid email yet I still paste it into every invoice" — is a different bug: `qbSendInvoice` built `invoicePayload` with `Line/TxnDate/PrivateNote/CustomerMemo/CustomerRef` but **no `BillEmail`**. QuickBooks does NOT auto-copy a customer's `PrimaryEmailAddr` onto an API-created invoice, so every invoice posted with a blank send-to and had to be typed by hand — even when the customer had an email. This is ALSO why the auto-send/AR-remind flow (which skips invoices with no `BillEmail`) couldn't send them. Fix: before the invoice POST, set `invoicePayload.BillEmail = {Address: email}` where email = `owner.Billing_Email || owner.Email`, falling back to the **owner's** QB customer `PrimaryEmailAddr` (never the billed sub-customer's — that could be a stray tenant address that then gets auto-emailed the pay link). Guards: only attach a ≤100-char well-formed address; if QuickBooks still rejects the POST **with** BillEmail, retry once **without** it so a bad email can NEVER block invoicing (warn either way). Preview now shows the same `Billing_Email || Email`. Purely additive to the send path — amounts/lines/CustomerRef/DocNumber untouched. Adversarially reviewed (no critical; the two Medium findings — QB-reject-blocks-invoice and sub-customer-fallback-mis-send — are both fixed here). Full suite green. Nesting note: adding unit sub-customers under a property created blank-email units (fixed by the backfill), but the pasting persisted on the property too because of THIS invoice-level gap — the property kept its email; the invoice just never carried it. NOTE: `/trash/invoice` (`2026-08-07`) still omits BillEmail — same one-line fix pending there.

**59. The email you see in the Hub is NOT proof it's in QuickBooks — the backfill now shows QuickBooks ground truth + lets you type-and-set any row (Aug 9, `2026-08-09.2`).** Brett hit this: 153 W Lanvale (Goldszmidt property) landed in **Skipped** even though the Hub shows `goldszmidtproperties@gmail.com` next to it. Cause: that email lives in the Hub/Sheets `Owners.Billing_Email`, but the QuickBooks **owner** customer has **no** `PrimaryEmailAddr`, so the backfill (which reads QuickBooks only) has nothing to copy down → skipped, no checkbox. Two additions: (a) preview now returns **`all_customers`** = every QB customer with the email read straight from QuickBooks (owners + properties + units), rendered in a filterable **"Every QuickBooks customer — the real email on file"** panel that prints **"(blank in QuickBooks)"** when QB truly has nothing — the answer to "how do I know which ones actually carry an email in QB." (b) An **explicit-set** mode: `POST /qb/backfill-emails {apply:true, email:"x@y.com", ids:[...]}` writes that exact address onto the chosen ids (validated, overwrites by design, chunked, confirm-gated). Fixes a skipped property directly, OR set it on the **owner** row then re-preview to cascade down via the normal inherit path. Explicit mode is a separate branch — it does not touch the inherit/force logic. The Hub→QuickBooks email disconnect (Sheets has it, QB doesn't) is the root lesson: **QB is its own store; a Hub email is not pushed to QB unless something writes it there.**

**56. Job photos/videos are made anyone-with-link readable AT UPLOAD, so vendors can view them without a Google login.** Uploaded WO media is owned by the Sheets service account; nothing made it link-viewable except a QB invoice send (which shares the WO folder for the customer memo). So a vendor opening a photo/video chip in the portal hit Google's sign-in / request-access wall — it was NEVER an issue of the vendor paying Google or their email being "in the system" (Drive files aren't shared per-email at all; they were simply private). Fix (`2026-08-08.1`): `driveShareAnyone(role:reader,type:anyone)` now runs at upload in BOTH paths — `handlePhotoUploadClean` (gated on `!isInternal`) and `logAttachment` (the vendor-portal resumable path, gated on `file_type ∉ NON_SHARE_FILE_TYPES`). **Vendor cost docs stay private:** `NON_SHARE_FILE_TYPES=['receipt','bill','invoice']` — never shared (preserves FEATURE_LOG rule 13; the `_Internal — Vendor Bills` folder is untouched). Backfill for pre-fix media = secret-gated `POST /admin/share-attachments` (`{dry_run?,limit?}` → `{scanned,shareable,shared,skipped_internal,skipped_no_id,failed,failures}`; idempotent — re-sharing a public file is harmless; skips `Active=FALSE` + the 3 cost types), surfaced as **Hub → Dev Log → 🖼 Fix photo/video sharing** (dry-run count → "Share them all"). Privacy note: this is the same "anyone-with-link" posture the invoice photo link already uses.

**55. The Optimizer loop's output reaches Brett via the BrettOS Command Center (pull), not push.** Scheduled-task push notifications don't surface on mobile yet, so background-agent output was landing in invisible sessions ("nothing popped up"). Fix: `command-center.html` now has an **Optimizer card** that pulls **read-only** `GET /ops-review-log?limit=N` (latest weekly-review rows) + `GET /ops-telemetry?days=7` and renders the review + telemetry. Both are admin-gated (WORKER_SECRET), read-only (no spend/write). The card fetch is best-effort + concurrent — it can NEVER trip the Command Center into sample mode or gate first paint; all dynamic text escaped via `h()`. **Delivery model of record:** the **Worker-cron Reviewer writes → Command Center reads** (reliable, mobile-friendly). **Scout & Prepare are Cowork sessions** with no secret/PAT and no reliable push — they surface **in-session** ("ask in chat: what've you got"), NOT via notification. Don't rebuild on push notifications until that surface works on mobile. Deployed `2026-08-07.12`.

**52. The weekly Optimizer Reviewer (B-129) runs as a WORKER CRON, not a Cowork task.** A fresh
scheduled Cowork session has no `WORKER_SECRET` and no `BRETT_GH_PAT` (verified Aug 7 — headless env
has only the permissionless GitHub tokens), so it can't read authed telemetry or the private repo.
The Worker cron can — it has Sheets + `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in env. Cron `"0 12 * * 1"` (Mon 12:00 UTC)
→ `scheduled()` branches on `event.cron` → `runWeeklyReview`: reads 7d of `Ops_Telemetry` → metrics +
stuck-pattern flags (H2) → `claude-sonnet-4-6` ranked proposal (best-effort; skipped under 5 rows) →
`Ops_Review_Log` tab → delivers ONLY if `digest_enabled=TRUE` (email still a stub, SMS needs Twilio,
so dormant today). **Do not** route `/ops-review` as GET (it spends + writes — it's `POST`, admin-gated)
and manual runs never deliver (only the cron delivers). On-demand: **Hub → Dev Log → 🔎 Run Ops Review**
(and 📊 Log test telemetry row to verify B-128 in one tap). The daily digest cron now also logs a
telemetry heartbeat row, so `Ops_Telemetry` self-creates every morning at 7am ET.
